Corrective substitution supplies the practical means by which the harmful state is exchanged for a serviceable one.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Restraint, Repulsion, and Repair","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_1cbc3dd0a7c06b859314","text":"A defective or absent function is restored by activation, compatible replacement, restraint, or exact repair.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Delegated Judgment and Credited Testimony","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_2013b795ca827c0ce001","text":"Trust grants standing to a witness or arbiter, delegated judgment gives that standing practical force, and attribution of truth or falsehood determines whether the entrusted word remains authoritative.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"D:Penetrating and Defective Judgment","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_20a488ca21e5eb9567f4","text":"Sound discernment penetrates a matter, while weak opinion fails to reach or govern it.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Debt, Dealing, and Rescission","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_20dc808389fbf1a8cbad","text":"Debt creates the continuing obligation, dealing establishes the relation between parties, and settlement governs disagreement within it. Rescission and assessed compensation provide two different closures: reversing the transaction or fixing what one party owes.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000148/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"branch_nomination","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"D:Ground Contact, Smoothness, and Sealed Surface","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_2790a8c7368f7366166e","text":"camel breast laid on the ground `ب ل د:B002/m02`, adhesion to the ground `ب ل د:B010/m01`, smooth even surface `خ ل ق:B008/m01`, sealed solid closure `خ ل ق:B012/m01`, patient bearing `ل ي س:B006/m01`","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Kinship, Return, and Protective Honor","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_330dfb02856ec1a1228c","text":"95:4 `خلقنا`; 95:5 `رددناه`; 95:6 `غير`; 95:7 `بعد`; 95:8 `أليس`","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Residence, City, and Civic Obedience","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_350b51bbeae5c2cf820e","text":"People remain in a settled place whose identity depends on residence, assembly, and obedience to authority.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000348/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"branch_nomination","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Delegated Judgment and Credited Testimony","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_37412bc6cc31a0fbea42","text":"delegated arbitration `ح ك م:B005/m01`, crediting testimony or oath `د ي ن:B007/m01`, assent and belief `ء م ن:B002/m01`, attribution of falsehood `ك ذ ب:B002/m01`","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"D:Exact and Beneficial Finish","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_39ed1b659eab2c75be9f","text":"95:4 `أحسن`; 95:6 `الصالحات`، `غير`، `أجر`; 95:7 `بعد`; 95:8 `أحكم`","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"D:Penetrating and Defective Judgment","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_3cf4a262479c147d2cf5","text":"95:4 `أحسن`; 95:7 `بعد`; 95:8 `أليس`، `أحكم`","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Residence, City, and Civic Obedience","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_4070a8c6bd4b47f90ae1","text":"A place becomes intelligible through prominent terrain, named stations, residence, and the civic order maintained there.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Residence, City, and Civic Obedience","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_461deb017d8833819959","text":"95:3 `البلد`; 95:4 `تقويم`; 95:7 `الدين`; 95:8 `أليس`","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Residence, City, and Civic Obedience","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_469da1bc81f59e5528c3","text":"Residence converts land into an inhabited station, prolonged remaining stabilizes it, and civic obedience gives the settlement an ordered political form. City is therefore both a place occupied and a relation maintained.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Negation, Exception, and Absence","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_4fc158e91b1a03338d7a","text":"Predicative negation denies a state, exception removes one member from a set, and otherness marks the excluded alternative. Negated distance reverses remoteness into proximity, while the empty-place formula gives negation a spatial result: no participant remains within the domain.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Adjudication and Final Reckoning","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_52aba70e1885d10038a1","text":"95:4 `خلقنا`، `تقويم`; 95:7 `الدين`; 95:8 `أحكم`، `الحاكمين`","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Bridle, Spear Forepart, and Handle","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_539033d9c1aa7fc8002d","text":"A handler orients and controls an implement or animal through a forepart, human-facing side, grip, and restraining fitting.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Rank, Aptitude, and Counsel","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_54ef4a7c20f4e2167d27","text":"Low rank and dull performance mark diminished standing, while penetrating counsel marks valued social capacity. position.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Worship, Obedience, and Upright
